1a:I[5340,["619","static/chunks/619-ba102abea3e3d0e4.js","526","static/chunks/app/course/%5Bkey%5D/%5Bch%5D/page-2f17e8c3cb8db837.js"],"default"]
18:Tc165c,<h1 id="上下文压缩">上下文压缩</h1>
<p>上下文压缩是指通过技术手段精简上下文内容，同时保证语义连贯。</p>
<p>上下文压缩的介入点主要有两个：</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>新消息防爆</strong>：需要立即处理的新消息</li>
<li><strong>上下文摘要</strong>：需要处理的旧消息</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="新消息防爆">新消息防爆</h2>
<blockquote>
<p>新消息防爆的处理特点是：</p>
<ol>
<li>针对的是新产生的消息</li>
<li>处理是立即发生的</li>
</ol>
<p>新消息代表着目前模型正在工作的内容，处理起来要非常谨慎，绝不能打破消息之间的因果关系。</p>
</blockquote>
<h3 id="工具返回防爆">工具返回防爆</h3>
<p>主要手段是：<strong>窗口控量，卸载兜底</strong></p>
<h4 id="窗口控量">窗口控量</h4>
<p>那些容易造成大结果的工具，需要通过参数让模型不要一次性拿完整结果。</p>
<p>比如<code>read_file</code>工具提供<code>offset</code>和<code>limit</code>，并给予了参数默认值</p>
<h4 id="卸载兜底">卸载兜底</h4>
<p>若工具返回超过了阈值，直接卸载。</p>
<div class="codehilite"><pre><span></span><code>[卸载后的结果] + [完整结果引用] → 模型
</code></pre></div>

<blockquote>
<p>卸载兜底功能已由<code>FilesystemMiddleware</code>中间件实现。</p>
<p>你可以配置工具的卸载阈值</p>
<p><code>python
FilesystemMiddleware(
  tool_token_limit_before_evict=20000
)</code></p>
<p>你可以配置特殊</p>
<p><code>python
backend=CompositeBackend(
  default=...,
  routes={},
  # 可配置 Deep Agent 内部产物的保存根目录
  artifacts_root="/home/user/workspace/.deepagents",
)</code></p>
</blockquote>
<h3 id="用户消息防爆">用户消息防爆</h3>
<p>用户消息超过阈值，直接卸载</p>
<blockquote>
<p>卸载兜底功能已由<code>FilesystemMiddleware</code>中间件实现。</p>
<p>你可以配置用户消息的卸载阈值</p>
<p><code>python
FilesystemMiddleware(
  human_message_token_limit_before_evict=50000
)</code></p>
</blockquote>
<div class="nb-cell"><div class="codehilite lang-nb"><pre><span></span><span class="kn">from</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nn">langgraph_sdk</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="kn">import</span> <span class="n">get_client</span>

<span class="n">client</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">get_client</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">url</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="s2">"http://127.0.0.1:2024"</span><span class="p">)</span>

<span class="n">assistant_id</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="s1">'bc46c028-583a-563c-8327-8ab22872af90'</span>
<span class="c1"># 创建新线程</span>
<span class="n">thread</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="k">await</span> <span class="n">client</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">threads</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">create</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">metadata</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">{</span><span class="s2">"__name__"</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s2">"用户消息卸载"</span><span class="p">})</span>
<span class="n">thread_id</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">thread</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"thread_id"</span><span class="p">]</span>

<div class="nb-out"><pre class="nb-result">(&#x27;01a05bd2-25aa-70d2-acef-7e8a51bf8382&#x27;,
 &#x27;bc46c028-583a-563c-8327-8ab22872af90&#x27;)</pre></div></div>
<div class="nb-cell"><div class="codehilite lang-nb"><pre><span></span><span class="n">resp</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="k">await</span> <span class="n">client</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">runs</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">wait</span><span class="p">(</span>
    <span class="nb">input</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">{</span>
        <span class="s2">"messages"</span><span class="p">:[</span>
            <span class="p">{</span>
                <span class="s2">"role"</span><span class="p">:</span><span class="s2">"user"</span><span class="p">,</span>
                <span class="s2">"content"</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s2">"</span><span class="se">\n</span><span class="s2">"</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">join</span><span class="p">([</span><span class="sa">f</span><span class="s2">"第</span><span class="si">{</span><span class="n">i</span><span class="si">}</span><span class="s2">条消息"</span> <span class="k">for</span> <span class="n">i</span> <span class="ow">in</span> <span class="nb">range</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mi">100000</span><span class="p">)])</span>
            <span class="p">}</span>
        <span class="p">]</span>
    <span class="p">},</span>
    <span class="n">thread_id</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">thread_id</span><span class="p">,</span> 
    <span class="n">assistant_id</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">assistant_id</span>
<span class="p">)</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
<blockquote>
<p>思考：</p>
<ol>
<li>系统提示词过大要不要立即处理？</li>
<li>工具参数过大要不要立即处理？</li>
<li>AI回复的普通消息要不要立即处理？</li>
</ol>
</blockquote>
<h2 id="上下文摘要">上下文摘要</h2>
<p>上下文摘要只处理旧消息，它会让旧的因果关系变得模糊。</p>
<p>但由于它是旧的因果关系，因此这种模糊对整个会话的影响较小。</p>
<h3 id="处理旧的特殊工具参数">处理旧的特殊工具参数</h3>
<p>在Agent运行期间，有两个特殊工具调用：</p>
<ul>
<li><code>write_file</code>：传入的新内容可能过大</li>
<li><code>edit_file</code>：传入的旧内容和新内容都可能过大</li>
</ul>
<p>这两个工具都有共同特点：</p>
<ul>
<li>工具的执行后，有价值的结果已落入文件</li>
<li>模型往往关心的是文件当下的状态，而工具的调用历史只代表改动过程</li>
<li>工具调用和工具返回的结果本身的因果关系就是暗淡的</li>
</ul>
<div class="codehilite"><pre><span></span><code><span class="n">summarization_middleware</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">SummarizationMiddleware</span><span class="p">(</span>
    <span class="n">model</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">model</span><span class="p">,</span>
    <span class="n">backend</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">backend</span><span class="p">,</span>

    <span class="c1"># 旧工具调用参数卸载配置</span>
    <span class="n">truncate_args_settings</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">{</span>
        <span class="c1"># 对话达到模型上下文窗口的 85% 时，开始检查旧工具调用参数</span>
        <span class="s2">"trigger"</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"fraction"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mf">0.85</span><span class="p">),</span>

        <span class="c1"># 最近 10% 的上下文中的工具参数保持完整</span>
        <span class="s2">"keep"</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"fraction"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mf">0.10</span><span class="p">),</span>

        <span class="c1"># 单个参数字符串超过 2000 个字符才卸载</span>
        <span class="s2">"max_length"</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="mi">2_000</span><span class="p">,</span>

        <span class="c1"># 卸载后的提示文字</span>
        <span class="s2">"truncation_text"</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s2">"...(argument truncated)"</span><span class="p">,</span>
    <span class="p">},</span>
<span class="p">)</span>
</code></pre></div>

<h3 id="处理整个上下文中的旧消息">处理整个上下文中的旧消息</h3>
<div class="codehilite"><pre><span></span><code><span class="n">summarization_middleware</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">SummarizationMiddleware</span><span class="p">(</span>
    <span class="n">model</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">model</span><span class="p">,</span>
    <span class="n">backend</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="n">backend</span><span class="p">,</span>

    <span class="c1"># 整个对话达到模型上下文窗口的 85% 时触发摘要</span>
    <span class="n">trigger</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"fraction"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mf">0.85</span><span class="p">),</span>

    <span class="c1"># 摘要时保留最近 10% 的上下文不参与摘要</span>
    <span class="n">keep</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="s2">"fraction"</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="mf">0.10</span><span class="p">),</span>
<span class="p">)</span>
</code></pre></div>
<div class="nb-cell"><div class="codehilite lang-nb"><pre><span></span><span class="n">assistant_id</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="s1">'58534c12-9bc2-5cd2-b195-be42dfcd1f1f'</span>
<span class="c1"># 创建新线程</span>
<span class="n">thread</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="k">await</span> <span class="n">client</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">threads</span><span class="o">.</span><span class="n">create</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">metadata</span><span class="o">=</span><span class="p">{</span><span class="s2">"__name__"</span><span class="p">:</span> <span class="s2">"历史上下文摘要"</span><span class="p">})</span>
<span class="n">thread_id</span> <span class="o">=</span> <span class="n">thread</span><span class="p">[</span><span class="s2">"thread_id"</span><span class="p">]</span>
<span class="n">thread_id</span><span class="p">,</span> <span class="n">assistant_id</span>
</pre></div>
